# Code Review: Logical-node `inspect` view

## Summary

This PR reworks `containerlab inspect` to show one entry per *topology node* by default, instead of one entry per container. Internally, `listContainers` now does a label-based listing of all lab containers, merges it with per-node topology listings (deduplicated by container ID), and applies a new `logicalNodeContainers` mapping that collapses internal netns holders and non-CPM SR-SIM components into their root node. Internal netns holders (SR Linux, SR-SIM) and tool containers (sshx, gotty) are hidden from the default and `--all` views; a new `--containers` flag restores the full per-container listing, and `--details` also shows every container. The filtering responsibility moves out of `PrintContainerInspect` into `cmd/deploy.go`, and new Robot suites plus unit tests cover the behavior. Docs are updated accordingly.

The core logic is well-constructed and correctly handles the edge cases I traced: CPM-A-present, CPM-B-only fallback, node-without-holder, internal tailscale node, ext-containers without labels, and exact-name filtering (the Docker runtime name filter is anchored `^name$`, so per-node listing returns exactly one container per topology node). The deploy summary behavior is preserved because `DeployResult.Containers` already contains one entry per topology node (CPM A for distributed SR-SIM, with forged management IPs from the holder).

No blocking defects found. Two minor items below.

## Findings

| Priority | Count | Summary |
|----------|-------|---------|
| P0 | 0 | — |
| P1 | 0 | — |
| P2 | 0 | — |
| P3 | 0 | — |
| P4 | 2 | `--containers` silently ignored on Clabernetes runtime; inconsistent yaml schema header between new test topologies |

### P4: `--containers` flag silently ignored with the Clabernetes lab runtime

**Location:** [head/cmd/inspect.go](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/8828fb63101726c57131c104c9636f4e43ab259c/cmd/inspect.go) — the early-return `if c.LabRuntime != nil { return c.ListLabRuntimeContainers(ctx, o.Destroy.All) }` at the top of `listContainers`.

**Problem:** The new `--containers` flag (added in `cmd/options.go` as `Inspect.Containers`) and the logical-node grouping are both applied after this early return. When `--runtime c9s` is used, the flag is silently ignored and the logical-node view is not applied at all — the lab-runtime path returns its own container list regardless of `o.Inspect.Containers`.

**Trigger:** Running `containerlab inspect --runtime c9s --containers` (or `--details` combined with the flag's intent) against a Clabernetes-managed lab.

**Impact:** Low. Clabernetes labs presumably have no internal netns holders, so the logical view may be a no-op there anyway, but the flag silently doing nothing is confusing, and the new "#### containers" section in [docs/cmd/inspect/index.md](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/8828fb63101726c57131c104c9636f4e43ab259c/docs/cmd/inspect/index.md) does not state that it is a local-runtime-only flag.

**Suggestion:** Either document in the "#### containers" section of `docs/cmd/inspect/index.md` that the flag applies to the local runtime only, or (better, if the lab-runtime listing can distinguish logical nodes) honor the flag in `ListLabRuntimeContainers` as well. The documentation note is the minimal proportionate correction.

### P4: Inconsistent yaml-language-server schema header between the two new test topologies

**Location:** [head/tests/02-basic-srl/05-inspect-views.clab.yml](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/8828fb63101726c57131c104c9636f4e43ab259c/tests/02-basic-srl/05-inspect-views.clab.yml#L1-L1) line 1.

**Problem:** The new `tests/13-srsim/15-inspect-views.clab.yml` begins with the `# yaml-language-server: $schema=...` schema comment (consistent with the repository's existing test topologies), while the sibling new file `tests/02-basic-srl/05-inspect-views.clab.yml` omits it.

**Trigger:** Opening the file in an editor with YAML schema validation, or diffing the two new files.

**Impact:** Cosmetic; editors lose schema-based autocompletion/validation for this file, and the two new files introduced by the same PR are inconsistent with each other.

**Suggestion:** Add the same `# yaml-language-server: $schema=...` header line to `tests/02-basic-srl/05-inspect-views.clab.yml` to match the sibling file and the rest of the test suite.

## Coverage Notes

- **Source-only review.** No tests were executed and no code was run; conclusions are based on static tracing of the head and base snapshots under `/source`.
- All 15 changed paths were reviewed: both workflow files, `cmd/deploy.go`, `cmd/inspect.go`, `cmd/inspect_test.go`, `cmd/options.go`, two docs pages, four new/modified test suites/resources, and `tests/common.robot`.
- `logicalNodeContainers` was traced against every unit-test vector in `cmd/inspect_test.go` (SR Linux with holder, distributed SR-SIM with CPM A, CPM-B-only fallback, internal tailscale node) and against real-world label assignments from `nodes/srl/netns.go`, `nodes/sros/netns.go`, and `nodes/sros/sros.go` (`calcComponentName`, `RootNodeName` on components and holders). Logic is correct in all traced cases, including ext-containers, which pass through as standalone nodes via the `key.node == ""` fallback.
- The `-t` topology filter (`DeleteFunc` keeping containers whose `NodeName` or `RootNodeName` is in `c.Nodes`) correctly retains netns holders (their `RootNodeName` is a topology node) while dropping tool containers, matching the documented `--topo` behavior.
- Deploy-summary correctness for distributed SR-SIM was verified by tracing `c.deploy` → `c.finalize` → `ListNodesContainers`, which yields exactly one container per topology node (CPM A, with holder-forged mgmt IPs); the test expectation `"linux sr-a"` in `tests/13-srsim/15-inspect-views.robot` is consistent.
- The relocated `Run Checked Pipeline` keyword in `tests/common.robot` is safely resolved because `tests/13-srsim/09-srsim-pause-container.robot` imports `../common.robot`; root-level `.robot` resource files are an established repository convention and are not picked up as suites since workflows invoke explicit file paths.
- Minor unverified detail: the Podman runtime's name filter (podman/util.go:708) was assumed to behave like the Docker runtime's exact-match filter; only the Docker implementation was fully read. The Podman CI path for the new suites runs `--all`/`-t` selectors, which do not depend on per-node name filtering.
- Performance: the `-t` path now performs one extra label-based `ListContainers` API call plus an `O(n·m)` dedup merge compared to the base single per-node listing. At realistic lab container counts this is negligible, and the label-based listing is what enables the `--containers` view without per-node queries, so this was not raised as a finding.
